### [68] [Explicit Layer Modeling for Video Object Insertion and Layer Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25802)
*Kyujin Han,Seungjoo Shin,Sunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriLayer大规模三元组视频数据集（含合成、背景、前景视频），并基于此构建DBL-Diffusion双分支扩散框架，实现显式分层视频表示，在物体插入和图层分解任务上显著优于隐式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑系统缺乏显式分层表示，导致合成、对象复用和一致性编辑受限；现有方法依赖隐式推理或逐场景优化，缺少显式前景层监督。

Method: 构建TriLayer数据集（包含对齐的合成、背景、前景三元组视频）；提出DBL-Diffusion双分支扩散框架，通过共享去噪和跨分支交互联合建模RGB合成和RGBA前景层；实例化为DBL-Insert（分层物体插入）和DBL-Decompose（视频图层分解）两个任务。

Result: 显式层建模显著提升了插入保真度和分解质量。

Conclusion: 显式前景层监督和双分支扩散框架能有效学习分层视频表示，克服隐式方法的局限性，实现更高质量的合成和分解。

Abstract: Most video editing systems still lack explicit layered video representations, limiting their ability to perform realistic compositing, object reuse, and consistent manipulation. This limitation is especially pronounced in video object insertion and video layer decomposition, where existing methods rely on implicit inference or per-scene optimization due to the absence of explicit foreground-layer supervision. We introduce TriLayer, a large-scale triplet video dataset containing aligned composite, background, and foreground videos, where the foreground layers include both object appearance and associated visual effects. This explicit supervision enables models to learn layered video representations directly rather than inferring them implicitly. Building on this dataset, we propose DBL-Diffusion, a dual-branch diffusion framework that jointly models RGB composites and RGBA foreground layers through shared denoising and cross-branch interaction. We instantiate the framework in two tasks: DBL-Insert for layered object insertion, which generates explicit RGBA layers for realistic compositing and flexible post-editing, and DBL-Decompose for video layer decomposition, which recovers foreground and background layers using triplet supervision. Experiments demonstrate that explicit layer modeling substantially improves both insertion fidelity and decomposition quality.

</details>


### [69] [Freq-RemoteVAR: Next-Frequency Autoregressive Modeling for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25815)
*Luqi Gong,Rui Xu,Yue Chen,Chao Li,Jingqi Hong,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Freq-RemoteVAR，一种基于频率自回归的遥感变化检测框架。该方法将变化检测重构为频域中的结构化生成问题，采用“从粗到细”的逐频率预测范式，通过频率感知掩码分词、频率 VAR Transformer、尺度对齐的RoPE交叉注意力（SRCA）和变化质量控制模块，在CDD、GZ-CD和LEVIR-CD数据集上优于现有方法，尤其在复杂外观变化和噪声干扰场景中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感变化检测方法多采用单步密集预测范式，直接从融合特征回归变化掩码，忽略了变化模式的内在频率特性，难以有效处理复杂外观变化和噪声干扰。

Method: 1) 频率感知掩码分词：通过傅里叶变换和量化将变化监督分解为多频率token目标；2) 频率VAR Transformer：对频率token进行因果自回归建模，从学习到的掩码查询开始，基于已生成token和双时相图像特征逐步预测频率级token；3) 尺度对齐的RoPE交叉注意力（SRCA）：在统一坐标系下对齐频域掩码查询与空域双时相特征，增强空间-频率一致性；4) 变化质量控制模块：通过动态归一化、注意力偏置和空间偏移调整自适应调节生成过程，抑制伪变化响应。

Result: 在CDD、GZ-CD和LEVIR-CD三个数据集上，Freq-RemoteVAR一致优于现有方法，尤其在复杂外观变化和噪声干扰的挑战性场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 将变化检测重构为频域中的结构化生成问题是有效且鲁棒的，频率自回归范式能够捕捉跨频率尺度的长程依赖，变化质量控制模块显著提升了模型对伪变化的抑制能力。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection aims to identify land-cover changes from bi-temporal images. Most existing methods follow a one-shot dense prediction paradigm, directly regressing a change mask from fused features. However, such approaches overlook the intrinsic frequency characteristics of change patterns. We propose Freq-RemoteVAR, a frequency autoregressive framework that reformulates change detection as a structured generation problem in the frequency domain. Instead of predicting the change mask in a single step, we introduce a next-frequency prediction paradigm, where change information is progressively generated from coarse to fine. We design a frequency-aware mask tokenization strategy that decomposes change supervision into multi-frequency token targets via Fourier transformation and quantization. We develop a Frequency VAR Transformer, which performs causal autoregressive modeling over frequency tokens. The model starts from learned mask queries and progressively predicts frequency-level tokens conditioned on previously generated tokens and bi-temporal image features, effectively capturing long-range dependencies across frequency scales. We introduce Scale-Aligned RoPE Cross Attention (SRCA) module, which aligns frequency-domain mask queries with spatial-domain bi-temporal features under a unified coordinate system, enhancing spatial-frequency consistency during generation. We propose a Change-quality Control module that adaptively modulates the generation process through dynamic normalization, attention biasing, and spatial offset adjustment, thereby suppressing pseudo-change responses and improving robustness. Extensive experiments on CDD, GZ-CD, and LEVIR-CD demonstrate that Freq-RemoteVAR consistently outperforms existing methods, particularly in challenging scenarios with complex appearance variations and noisy disturbances.

</details>


### [70] [SepPrune:A Separator-based Pruning Framework for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25818)
*Yuchen Wang,Qihui Zhu,Yang Liu,Xiaoyan Sun,Siying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）高分辨率输入产生大量视觉token导致计算成本高昂的问题，提出SepPrune，一种无需训练、即插即用的剪枝方法，利用模态分隔符token作为统一查询来排序和选择信息性视觉token，在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上去除80.2%视觉token的同时保留96.3%的原始精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM（如Qwen2.5-VL、InternVL3）为高分辨率输入生成大量视觉token，导致计算成本巨大。现有视觉token剪枝方法存在两类局限：一是依赖跨模态注意力，无法在prefill阶段前进行剪枝；二是依赖多样性估计，计算开销高。因此需要一种高效、可提前剪枝的低开销方法。

Method: 观察到视觉和文本token的注意力分数均在模态分隔符token处达到峰值，表明分隔符桥接两种模态。基于此，提出SepPrune：将分隔符token作为统一查询，对所有视觉token进行注意力排序，选择信息量最高的视觉token保留，其余剪枝。该方法复用LLM内置投影参数，无需架构修改，无需训练，即插即用。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，SepPrune达到SOTA性能：去除80.2%的视觉token，仅保留96.3%的原始精度。

Conclusion: SepPrune是一种高效、无需训练、即插即用的视觉token剪枝方法，通过利用模态分隔符token作为查询，在不改变模型架构的前提下大幅减少计算开销，同时保持高精度。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs), such as Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3, generate large numbers of vision tokens for high-resolution inputs, leading to substantial computational cost. Existing vision token pruning methods either depend on cross-modal attention and cannot prune before the prefill stage, or rely on diversity estimation with high computational overhead. We observe that attention scores from both vision and text tokens peak at modality separator tokens, suggesting that these separators bridge the two modalities. Based on this observation, we propose SepPrune, an efficient, training-free, plug-and-play pruning method that uses the separator token as a unified query to rank and select informative vision tokens. SepPrune reuses the LLM's built-in projection parameters and requires no architectural changes. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that SepPrune achieves state-of-the-art performance, retaining 96.3% of the original accuracy while removing 80.2% of vision tokens.

</details>


### [71] [Food Image Segmentation with LLM-Derived Ingredient Labels and Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25820)
*Jui-Feng Chi,Wei-Ta Chu,Sheng-Long Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两个即插即用的多模态模块（LIM-F和LIM-Q），通过将大语言模型推断的食材标签注入视觉分析流程，在不需预对齐文本与图像的情况下，显著提升了食品图像分割性能，在FoodSeg103基准上达到SOTA（mIoU 55.0），且仅适度增加训练GPU内存消耗（最多3.8 GB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品图像分割模型在视觉相似的食材和稀有食品类别上性能不佳，亟需一种能利用食材语义信息提升分割精度的方法。

Method: 提出两个即插即用模块：LIM-F（语言注入模块-特征）适配多层级输出的图像编码器（如Swin Transformer），LIM-Q（语言注入模块-查询）适配Mask2Former风格的Transformer解码器；利用大语言模型从食品图像中推断食材标签，将语义信息直接注入视觉分析管线，无需图像-文本预对齐。

Result: 在FoodSeg103上，LIM-Q+Swin-L+Mask2Former达mIoU 55.0（SOTA）；LIM-F在同配置下达54.4 mIoU；LIM-F在CNN架构中从47.7提升至49.8 mIoU；训练GPU内存最多增加3.8 GB。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了实用且可扩展的细粒度食品理解方案，在性能提升与资源消耗之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Food image segmentation plays a vital role in health-related applications such as nutrition tracking and personalized health monitoring. However, existing models often underperform on visually similar ingredients and rare food categories. To address this issue, we propose two plug-and-play multimodal modules that enhance the segmentation performance by leveraging ingredient labels inferred from food images using large language models (LLMs). The first module, called LIM-F (Language Injection Module for Features), is designed to pair with any image encoder that produces multi-layer outputs (e.g., Swin Transformer), while the second module, LIM-Q (Language Injection Module for Queries), targets Mask2Former-style Transformer-based decoders. Both modules enable training without the need for pre-aligning images with text by directly injecting semantic ingredient information into the visual analysis pipeline. On the FoodSeg103 benchmark, the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance. Specifically, integrating LIM-Q into the Mask2Former decoder with a Swin-L image encoder yields a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 55.0. LIM-F also demonstrates strong generalization and competitive performance, reaching an mIoU of 54.4 under the same model (Swin-L+Mask2Former). Furthermore, its applicability extends beyond Transformer-based decoders, as evidenced by an improvement from 47.7 to 49.8 mIoU when integrated into a CNN-based architecture. Notably, the improved segmentation accuracy is achieved with only a moderate (at most 3.8 GB) increase in the GPU memory consumption during training. Thus, the proposed approach offers a practical and scalable solution for fine-grained food understanding.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Static Costs: Learning-Dynamics Aware Loss Functions for Long-Tailed Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25830)
*Varad Shinde,Nikhil Kumar Shrey,Magesh Rajasekaran,Md Saiful Islam Sajol,Harshil Bhargava,Subhajit Sidanta,Supratik Mukhopadhyay,Yimin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习动态感知损失函数(LDAL)，通过动态调整类别权重而非静态频率，解决长尾数据集中的类别不平衡问题，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重加权方法通常依赖静态类别频率来惩罚模型，忽略了网络随时间实际学习某个类别的动态有效性，导致模型在尾部类别上表现不佳。

Method: LDAL框架通过三个机制连续调整类别权重：(i)学习到的特征表示强度（语义尺度）；(ii)基于预测香农熵衡量的每个类别的内在学习难度；(iii)追踪连续epoch间预测变化的epoch间正则化项，以稳定训练并避免局部极小值。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，LDAL显著超越了当前最先进的重加权损失函数，在准确性和泛化能力之间实现了最优平衡，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LDAL作为一种纯目标函数，通过动态适应模型的特征学习过程，有效解决了长尾分布下的类别不平衡问题，为实际应用提供了高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models in computer vision face significant challenges when trained on long-tailed datasets, where a few majority classes dominate while many minority classes are severely underrepresented. Such imbalances frequently arise in real-world scenarios such as rare species recognition, manufacturing fault detection, and medical image understanding, leading to biased models that underperform on tail classes. Existing reweighting methods typically rely on static class frequencies to penalize the model, ignoring the dynamic nature of how effectively a network actually learns a class over time. We address this by introducing a novel Learning-Dynamics Aware Loss (LDAL) function that shifts the focus from static sample counts to dynamic learning progress. LDAL framework adjusts class weights continuously by leveraging: (i) the strength of learned feature representations (semantic scale), (ii) the intrinsic learning difficulty of each class, measured via the Shannon entropy of its predictions, and (iii) an inter-epoch regularizer term that tracks prediction shifts between consecutive epochs to stabilize training and avoid local minima. LDAL is purely a objective function which incurs negligible computational overhead while adapting to the feature learning of the model. Experimental results on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly surpasses state-of-the-art reweighting loss functions, providing an optimal trade-off between accuracy and generalizability. The source code is available at https://github.com/sdm2026/ldal

</details>


### [73] [Adversarial Deepfake Generation and an Investigation of Purification-Based Adversarial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25842)
*Junghyun Kim,Seunghyun Kim,Jiyoung Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了团队在ImageCLEF 2026深度伪造检测与生成任务中的工作：图像生成任务采用FLUX.1-dev+PuLID身份保持人脸合成与多模型PGD对抗攻击（联合12个检测器，含DiffJPEG-in-loop、MI/DI/EoT、自适应加权、两阶段热启动），达成90%的组委会检测器逃逸率和57.6%的参与者检测器逃逸率，最终生成得分为0.4170；图像检测任务采用SigLIP+DINOv2（AI生成图像）和GenD-DINOv3（人脸篡改）的最大概率集成，在基线深度伪造上达到99.4%准确率但对真实图像假阳性高，最终检测得分为0.6986；此外自研了基于纯化的对抗检测方法，发现EFFORT检测器下的中值-3纯化原始|Δlogit|在四种对抗源上AUROC达0.81-0.98，推翻了简单主干保留假说，并揭示了Q70处信号崩溃的JPEG质量悬崖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与ImageCLEF 2026深度伪造检测与生成任务，探索高效的身份保持人脸合成与对抗攻击方法，以及鲁棒的深度伪造检测方案；同时自研纯化对抗检测机制，检验CLIP ViT-L/14主干在对抗检测中的可迁移性假设。

Method: 生成任务：FLUX.1-dev+PuLID身份保持人脸合成，结合多模型PGD对抗攻击（同时攻击12个检测器，包含DiffJPEG-in-loop、MI/DI/EoT动量/多样性/期望变换、自适应损失加权、两阶段热启动策略）。检测任务：SigLIP+DINOv2（检测AI生成图像）与GenD-DINOv3（检测人脸篡改）的最大概率集成。自研纯化对抗检测：比较三种检测信号家族，聚焦EFFORT检测器下中值-3纯化后的原始|Δlogit|信号。

Result: 生成任务：90%逃逸率（组委会检测器）、57.6%逃逸率（参与者检测器），生成得分0.4170。检测任务：基线深度伪造99.4%准确率，但对真实图像假阳性高，检测得分0.6986。纯化对抗检测：EFFORT检测器下|Δlogit|在四种对抗源上AUROC达0.81-0.98；发现JPEG压缩在Q70处信号崩溃的尖锐阈值。

Conclusion: 提出的多模型PGD对抗攻击在生成任务中有效；检测集成虽准确率高但假阳性问题突出需改进；纯化对抗检测方法有效区分对抗与干净输入，推翻了简单主干保留假说，并揭示了JPEG压缩质量悬崖（Q70）对检测信号的关键影响。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of team "Go To Germany" in the ImageCLEF 2026 Deepfake Detection and Generation Task. For the image generation task, we employ FLUX.1-dev with PuLID for identity-preserving face synthesis, combined with a multi-model PGD adversarial attack targeting 12 detectors simultaneously (DiffJPEG-in-loop, MI/DI/EoT, adaptive weighting, two-stage warm-start). Our approach achieved 90% evasion against organizer detectors and 57.6% against participant detectors, with a final generation score of 0.4170. For the image detection task, we combine two complementary detectors - SigLIP+DINOv2 for AI-generated images and GenD-DINOv3 for face manipulations - in a max-probability ensemble, achieving 99.4% accuracy on baseline deepfakes but suffering from high false-positive rates on real images, resulting in a final detection score of 0.6986. Beyond the official submission, we conducted a self-initiated investigation of purification-based adversarial detection, comparing three families of detection signals across six detectors that share a CLIP ViT-L/14 backbone. We find that raw $|Δ\text{logit}|$ under median-3 purification, applied through the EFFORT detector, separates adversarial inputs from clean inputs with AUROC 0.81-0.98 across four adversarial source types - a finding that refutes the simple backbone-preservation hypothesis and exposes a sharp JPEG-quality cliff at Q70 where the signal collapses.

</details>


### [74] [Open-Ended CT Volume Segmentation with Weak Supervision from Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25860)
*Sanjay Subramanian,Junwei Yu,Zirui Wang,Rohil Malpani,Maggie Chung,Adam Yala,Dan Klein,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合体素级强监督和切片级弱监督（来自放射报告）的CT文本条件分割训练方法，通过在SAM3模型上联合使用标准分割损失和切片级分类损失，在ReXGroundingCT数据集上实现了分割Dice分数的显著提升（从1000个全标注体积时的8%相对增益到250个时的22%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT扫描的文本条件分割模型训练中，体素级标注成本高昂且难以规模化，而放射报告中包含大量可用的切片级弱监督信息。作者旨在利用这种粗粒度但可扩展的监督信号来提升分割性能，尤其是在全标注数据稀缺的场景下。

Method: 从大规模的CT扫描-放射报告对数据库中提取描述性发现及其对应的切片索引。基于通用2D图像分割模型SAM3进行微调，联合使用：1）来自强标注数据的标准分割损失；2）来自提取的弱监督信息的切片级分类损失。

Result: 在ReXGroundingCT数据集上，该方法显著提升了分割Dice分数。当全标注体积数量从1000减少到250时，相对增益从8%提高到22%，表明弱监督策略在标注稀缺时尤为有效。

Conclusion: 结合切片级文本弱监督可以有效提升CT文本条件分割模型的性能，特别是在强标注数据有限的场景下，为降低医学图像分割对密集标注的依赖提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce a method for training a text-conditioned segmentation model for CT scans, which combines voxel-level supervision with coarse but scalable slice-level supervision from reports. We extract, from a large database of scan-report pairs, descriptions of findings with indices of slices where those findings occur. We then finetune a general-purpose 2D image segmentation model, SAM3, with standard segmentation losses from strongly labeled data and with a slice-level classification loss from the extracted weak supervision. Our results on the ReXGroundingCT dataset illustrate that this strategy improves the segmentation dice score: from an 8% relative gain when there are 1000 fully labeled volumes to 22% when there are 250 fully labeled volumes.

</details>


### [75] [TIGA: Trajectory-Injected Generative Attack against Black-box AIGC Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25894)
*Xia Du,Zhuosen Bao,Zheng Lin,Jizhe Zhou,Jiawei Lian,Chi-man Pun,Jun Luo,Wei Ni,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Trajectory-Injected Generative Attack (TIGA)，一种无需源图像、无需重新训练扩散模型的对抗攻击框架，通过在单一 DDIM 采样轨迹中注入对抗方向，生成能够逃避黑盒 AIGC 检测器的合成人脸图像，在保持高感知质量的同时实现强黑盒攻击性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型生成的人脸图像高度逼真，对 AIGC 取证检测器构成挑战；现有的逃避方法通常需要扰动预生成图像或依赖检测器感知的训练，可能引入可见/统计伪影，且在扩散模型必须冻结、目标检测器仅黑盒可访问的场景下适用性受限。

Method: TIGA 在单一 DDIM 采样轨迹中：(1) 从多个白盒代理检测器聚合梯度，形成可迁移的符号感知先验；(2) 使用对称有限差分查询进行各向异性方向搜索，估计黑盒目标响应；(3) 通过衰减动量稳定估计方向；(4) 按照 DDIM 噪声调度注入对抗方向；(5) 使用频域重塑抑制高频伪影。

Result: 在代理检测器和未见过的专用取证检测器上，TIGA 实现了强黑盒攻击性能、良好的可迁移性，以及在常见后处理操作下的高鲁棒性，同时保持高感知质量，且无需源图像或扩散模型重新训练。

Conclusion: TIGA 是一种源图像无关、无需训练的攻击框架，通过在生成过程中而非生成后注入对抗特性，有效绕过了黑盒 AIGC 检测器，具有良好的实用性、迁移性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent diffusion models have achieved remarkable realism in facial image synthesis, posing growing challenges to artificial intelligence-generated content (AIGC) forensic detectors.Existing evasion methods typically perturb pre-generated images or require detector-aware training, which may introduce visible or statistical artifacts and limit applicability when the diffusion model must remain frozen and the target detector is accessible only through black-box queries. We propose Trajectory-Injected Generative Attack (TIGA), a source-image-free and training free framework that generates detector-evasive images within a single diffusion sampling trajectory. TIGA steers the latent Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) trajectory so that adversarial properties emerge during generation rather than being added afterward. TIGA first aggregates gradients from multiple white-box surrogate detectors to form a transferable, sign-aware prior, and then performs anisotropic directional search with symmetric finite-difference queries to estimate the black-box target response. The estimated directions are stabilized by decayed momentum and injected according to the DDIM noise schedule, with frequency-domain reshaping to suppress high frequency artifacts. Experiments on surrogate and unseen specialized forensic detectors show that TIGA achieves strong blackbox attack performance, transferability, and high robustness under common post-processing operations without source images or diffusion-model retraining, while preserving high perceptual quality.

</details>


### [76] [Evaluating VLMs for Autonomous Agent-Driven Geometry Clipping Detection in Video Game QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25921)
*Carlos Celemin,Benedict Wilkins,Adrián Barahona-Ríos,Saman Zadtootaghaj,Nabajeet Barman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究了VLM在游戏QA管线中检测几何裁剪异常的能力，六种模型在零样本条件下表现各异，Gemini表现最佳，但所有模型都存在大量误报，建议作为高召回率候选过滤器而非独立检测器使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索VLM能否在游戏QA的几何裁剪异常检测任务中发挥作用，以减少人工标注成本并提升自动化水平。

Method: 设计了一个agent驱动的游戏QA管线，使用自定义探索agent收集视觉观察，自动标注管线提供帧级裁剪标签。在零样本提示设置下测试了六种VLM，并分析了四种提示变体的敏感度。

Result: VLM能捕捉几何裁剪相关的视觉线索，但所有模型都在视觉模糊帧（如近接触几何和部分遮挡）上产生大量误报；Gemini-3.1-Flash在准确性和鲁棒性上最优；开源模型的精确率和召回率随提示设计大幅波动。

Conclusion: 当前VLM更适合作为多阶段QA管线中的高召回率候选过滤器，而非独立的bug检测器。

Abstract: In this work, we study the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for anomaly detection in an agent-driven game Quality Assurance (QA) pipeline focusing on geometry clipping. In this evaluation, a custom exploration agent navigates a game level to collect visual observations, while the automatic annotation pipeline provides frame-level clipping labels. This setup allows us to evaluate recent VLMs on a controlled anomaly detection task without manual annotation. We benchmark six recent VLMs (Gemini, GPT, Qwen, Gemma, Llama, and Ministral) under a zero-shot prompting setting and analyse their sensitivity to four prompt variants.
  Our results show that while the VLMs can capture visual cues associated with geometry clipping, they all produce substantial false positives on visually ambiguous frames such as near-contact geometry and partial occlusions. Gemini-3.1-Flash achieves the best overall accuracy and is the most robust to prompt variation, while open-source models exhibit large precision--recall swings depending on the prompt design. These findings suggest that current VLMs are best suited as high-recall candidate filters within multi-stage QA pipelines rather than as standalone bug detectors.

</details>


### [77] [Face De-Identification: A Domain-Centric Survey from Capture to Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25926)
*Hui Wei,Hao Yu,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次统一综述了人脸去识别（Face De-ID）在物理域、传感器域和数字域三大数据采集环节的研究进展，系统分析了各领域的方法、挑战和评估协议，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据隐私和负责任AI日益受到重视，人脸去识别成为跨计算机视觉和隐私保护领域的重要研究方向，但缺乏涵盖完整数据采集管线的统一综述。

Method: 采用"领域中心"视角，将人脸去识别方法按数据采集流程分为物理域（可穿戴设备/材料）、传感器域（集成隐私机制）和数字域（像素/外观级后处理）三类，系统分析各域方法、挑战及评估协议。

Result: 识别出各领域特有的技术挑战，指出了当前评估协议缺乏标准化和综合性基准的问题，并汇总了相关数据集和开源代码资源。

Conclusion: 本文为从业者提供了人脸去识别领域的全景视图，强调了跨域方法整合和标准化评估的重要性，并明确了若干关键开放问题和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Face de-identification (De-ID) aims to remove or conceal personally identifiable facial features in images or videos to prevent identity recognition while preserving utility for downstream tasks. With the rising emphasis on data privacy and responsible AI, face De-ID has emerged as an active research area spanning computer vision and privacy-preserving communities. Early approaches, and many contemporary ones, operate in the digital domain by modifying pixel-level or appearance-level features through post-capture processing. Recent advances extend face De-ID beyond post-processing by integrating privacy mechanisms directly into sensors during image acquisition, bridging sensing systems and downstream vision algorithms. In parallel, physical-domain methods explore wearable accessories and materials that conceal identity information in real-world environments prior to capture. In this survey, we present the first unified overview that spans the full data acquisition pipeline, encompassing the physical, sensor, and digital domains. Through this domain-centric lens, we systematically analyze current methodologies, technical progress, and the distinct challenges inherent to each stage. We then review and organize existing evaluation protocols, examining current practices and highlighting the critical need for standardized, comprehensive benchmarks. Finally, we identify key open problems and outline emerging research directions to guide future work in this rapidly evolving field. To support ongoing research, we maintain a project page that organizes relevant literature with collected datasets and open source code: https://github.com/CV-AC/Awesome-FaceDe-ID.

</details>


### [78] [MODUS: Decoder-Only Any-to-Any Modeling of Diverse Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25948)
*Mingqiao Ye,Zhaochong An,Zhitong Gao,Xian Liu,François Fleuret,Chuan Li,Amir Zadeh,Serge Belongie,Afshin Dehghan,Jesse Allardice,David Mizrahi,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Amir Zamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Modus，一种仅用解码器的任意到任意多模态模型，无需特定模态的头部、损失函数或任务流程，在多种基准上达到与专业模型和多任务基线竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的任意到任意多模态模型通常从头训练，使用编码器-解码器或扩散架构，这限制了性能并无法利用强大的预训练仅解码器模型作为先验知识。

Method: 采用仅解码器架构，对称地处理所有模态，支持任意模态作为输入和输出，无需特定模态的头部、损失函数或任务流水线。

Result: Modus在多种基准上表现出色，与专业模型和多任务基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 仅解码器的任意到任意多模态建模是一种有效方法，可支持链式生成、跨模态自验证等多种应用，且具有开箱即用的强大性能。

Abstract: Any-to-any models predict any modality from any combination of others within a single network, a formulation used in multimodal vision and vision-language models, and increasingly in scientific domains such as ecology and astronomy. Existing any-to-any models are typically trained from scratch using encoder-decoder or diffusion architectures, impacting their performance and preventing them from using strong pre-trained decoder-only models as a prior. In this work, we investigate decoder-only any-to-any multimodal modeling, which treats all modalities symmetrically and supports arbitrary modalities as inputs and outputs without modality-specific heads, losses, or task pipelines. Because every modality is both an input and an output of the same model, the resulting model, named Modus, can support a range of applications, such as chained generation through intermediate modalities or cross-modal self-verification by scoring the model's own outputs with another generated modality. Modus demonstrates strong out-of-the-box performance and is competitive with specialist and multitask baselines using a single model across various benchmarks. All materials are open-sourced at https://modus-multimodal.epfl.ch/.

</details>


### [79] [Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Video-Level Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25961)
*Podakanti Satyajith Chary,Barath Parthiban,Pranesh Velmurugan,Adeeba Khan,Nagarajan Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM-AH是一个用于识别视频中矛盾/犹豫情绪的多模态框架，通过对齐冻结的视觉、音频和文本编码器，结合流式跨模态不协调评分、下窗口预测、行为原型发现和元数据条件化，并利用知识引导的大语言模型进行推理，在525个视频的测试集上达到macro F1 0.6133，远超零样本基线0.2827。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矛盾/犹豫(A/H)是阻碍健康行为改变的冲突性情感状态，在视频层面识别困难，因为信号来自面部、声音、语言和肢体等多模态之间的不一致，且个体间表现差异大。

Method: PRISM-AH框架包含：(1) 将冻结的视觉、音频和文本编码器对齐到短时间窗口；(2) 轻量级流式模型进行跨模态不协调评分、预测下一窗口以暴露犹豫惊讶信号、发现行为原型，并基于参与者元数据条件化；(3) 密集窗口级标注作为辅助目标监督，决策阈值针对macro F1校准；(4) 知识引导的LLM基于结构化证据和数据集专家线索分类法进行推理，其判决在验证性能提升时进行晚期融合。

Result: 在525个视频的标注公开测试集上，PRISM-AH的macro F1达到0.6133，而零样本基线为0.2827。推理增益从验证集成功迁移到更大的测试集。

Conclusion: PRISM-AH有效解决了多模态矛盾/犹豫情绪识别问题，显著优于零样本基线，且验证了知识引导的LLM推理能够带来可迁移的性能提升。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are conflicting affective states that precede the delay or abandonment of health behaviour change. Recognition of A/H at the video level is difficult, since the signal arises from disagreement across and within facial, vocal, linguistic, and bodily modalities, and manifests differently across individuals. The proposed PRISM-AH (Predictive Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Multimodal Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition), is a framework that treats A/H as a multimodal conflict that unfolds over time. Frozen vision, audio, and text encoders are aligned into short time windows and passed to a lightweight streaming model that scores cross-modal dissonance, predicts each next window to expose a hesitation surprise signal, discovers behaviour prototypes, and is conditioned on participant metadata. Dense window-level annotations supervise the model as an auxiliary objective, and the decision threshold is calibrated for macro F1. A knowledge-guided large language model then reasons over structured evidence using the expert cue taxonomy of the dataset, and its verdict is fused late only when validation performance improves. On the labelled public test partition of 525 videos, PRISM-AH attains a macro F1 of 0.6133, compared to the reported zero-shot baseline of 0.2827. The reasoning gain is validated to transfer from validation to the larger test partition.

</details>


### [80] [LaP-Forensics: Latent-Pixel Consistency Guided Multimodal Reasoning for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25962)
*Can Wang,Yuhao Wang,Yushe Cao,Canran Xiao,Fei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaP-Forensics提出多模态框架，结合RGB图像与Stable Diffusion DDIM重建残差图进行伪造检测与定位，通过GRPO优化生成文本分析与伪影掩码，在跨生成器检测和伪影定位任务上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型产生的图像视觉伪影越来越难以察觉，导致仅依赖表面外观的检测器和解释方法性能下降，需要融合重建级取证线索来提升检测能力。

Method: 使用冻结的Stable Diffusion DDIM反转-重建模型提供固定重建参考，计算残差图衡量局部一致性；独立投影器编码RGB图像和残差图后，通过Where-What-Why模型预测文本分析和伪影掩码；采用监督微调+GRPO优化，奖励函数结合掩码重叠、输出结构及证据参考项；另设图像级头融合RGB和DDIM残差类别特征。

Result: 在UniversalFakeDetect数据集上实现跨生成器检测，在SynthScars官方基准上取得有竞争力的伪影定位结果。通过线索构建、反转步数、组件、奖励项及反事实分析验证了残差流的有效性。

Conclusion: 残差流在设定评估场景下对伪造检测有贡献，但自由形式文本的忠实性和后处理下的可靠性仍是开放局限性。

Abstract: Recent generative models can produce images with few obvious visual artifacts, weakening detectors and explanations that rely only on surface appearance. We present LaP-Forensics, a multimodal framework that augments RGB semantics with reconstruction-based forensic evidence. A frozen Stable Diffusion DDIM inversion-reconstruction model provides a fixed reconstruction reference, and its residual map measures local compatibility with that reference. Independent projectors encode the RGB image and residual map before a structured Where-What-Why model predicts a textual analysis and an artifact mask.Supervised fine-tuning is followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), whose reward combines mask overlap with output-structure and evidence-reference terms. These text-side terms encourage the model to refer to the consistency map but do not constitute a verifier of free-form textual truth. A separate image-level head fuses RGB and DDIM-residual class features. Experiments show cross-generator detection on UniversalFakeDetect and competitive artifact localization on the official SynthScars benchmark. Controlled cue-construction, inversion-horizon, component, reward-term, and counterfactual analyses support the utility of the residual stream under the evaluated settings, while free-form textual faithfulness and reliability under post-processing remain open limitations.

</details>


### [81] [Quasi-SVD: Learning a Lie-constrained matrix factorisation for real-time imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25967)
*Christopher Hahne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Quasi-SVD，一种可微、全并行化的GPU矩阵分解框架。通过严格正交一个Lie参数化因子+软约束恢复其余分量，避免迭代优化，在医学成像任务上实现3-20x加速和>25 FPS吞吐量，重建SSIM达0.89-0.94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SVD算法本质上是串行的，限制了GPU实时吞吐量，难以部署在临床在线流程中。医学成像应用日益需要实时处理，亟需可并行化的矩阵分解方案。

Method: 提出Quasi-SVD框架：(1) 采用非对称设计，对单个Lie参数化因子保证严格正交性；(2) 通过软约束恢复其余分量；(3) 完全并行化，无需迭代奇异向量优化；(4) 可微架构支持端到端训练。

Result: (1) 重建保真度SSIM=0.89-0.94；(2) 相比cuSOLVER和随机SVD实现3-20x加速；(3) 吞吐量超过25 FPS；(4) 在超声定位显微镜（高维矩阵）和穆勒矩阵偏振测量（小矩阵批量处理）两项医学成像任务中均验证有效。

Conclusion: Quasi-SVD优先考虑下游重建保真度而非精确谱恢复，使结构化矩阵分解在实时成像中变得实用。在临床矩阵规模下吞吐量超过25 FPS，足以支持实时图像引导工作流，这是经典求解器目前无法支持的。

Abstract: Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) underlies matrix factorisation tasks across computational imaging, with medical applications increasingly demanding real-time processing. Yet SVD algorithms are inherently sequential, constraining real-time GPU throughput and limit online deployment in clinical pipelines. This study introduces Quasi-SVD, a differentiable, fully parallelized matrix factorization framework for GPUs. Rather than enforcing orthogonality on both factors, it guarantees exact orthogonality for a single Lie-parameterized factor while recovering the remaining components through soft constraints, enabling efficient parallel decomposition without iterative singular-vector optimization. This asymmetric design, provably sufficient for valid factorisation, achieves reconstruction fidelity of SSIM = 0.89-0.94 and accelerates computation by 3-20x relative to cuSOLVER and randomised SVD, enabling throughput above 25 FPS. Performance is evaluated on two medical imaging tasks spanning complementary computational regimes: (1) spatio-temporal background subtraction for ultrasound localisation microscopy, requiring high-dimensional matrix separation, and (2) Mueller matrix polarimetry for neurosurgical tissue characterisation, requiring massive batch processing of small matrices. Across both regimes and multiple imaging instruments, the proposed framework demonstrates robust domain transfer and throughput exceeding 25 FPS at clinical matrix scales, a rate sufficient for live image-guided workflows that classical solvers cannot currently support in these settings. By prioritising downstream reconstruction fidelity over exact spectral recovery, Quasi-SVD makes structured matrix factorisation practical for real-time imaging.

</details>


### [82] [Schrödinger's Cat: Probabilistic Representation and Prediction of Potential Scene Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25984)
*Timy Phan,Jannik Wiese,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GARFIELD是一种概率场景运动预测模型，通过学习结构化的时空潜变量分布，实现对多模态未来轨迹的联合采样与高效密度估计，相比大规模视频生成模型速度提升97倍，密度估计快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么生成外观主导的视频预测，要么仅采样少量轨迹而未能显式建模可能运动的分布，缺乏对多模态未来场景演变的高效概率建模能力。

Method: 提出GARFIELD模型，学习场景运动学的结构化时空潜变量分布，包含联合轨迹采样模块和确定性密度解码器，支持通过额外稀疏约束逐步细化不确定性。

Result: 运动规划性能与大型视频生成模型相当，轨迹采样速度提升97倍；运动密度估计比蒙特卡洛采样快两个数量级，支持交互式探索和不确定性感知规划。

Conclusion: GARFIELD通过结构化潜变量分布实现了对场景未来运动的高效概率建模，在保持竞争力的同时大幅提升计算效率，为交互式不确定性感知规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Predicting how a scene may evolve from partial observations requires reasoning about multiple possible futures rather than committing to a single trajectory. Existing approaches either generate appearance-dominated video predictions or sample a small number of trajectories without explicitly modeling the distribution of possible motion. We introduce Goal-Aware Representations of Future kInEmatic Latent Distributions (GARFIELD), a probabilistic model of scene kinematics that learns a structured spatio-temporal latent representation of the distribution over possible futures given an image and optional spatio-temporally sparse constraints. The same latent representation enables both joint sampling of all trajectories and direct access to the underlying motion distribution through an efficient deterministic density decoder. As a result, uncertainty about future motion can be localized to specific scene elements and timesteps and progressively refined through additional constraints. Experiments demonstrate strong motion planning performance competitive with large video generation models while sampling trajectories $97\times$ faster. Our method further estimates motion densities two orders of magnitude faster than Monte-Carlo sampling from motion generation models, enabling interactive exploration and uncertainty-aware planning.

</details>


### [83] [On the Use of Synthetic Data for Threshold Calibration in Face Recognition: Performance and Security Implications for Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25990)
*Arto Apila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在欧盟EES边境管控系统中使用合成人脸数据进行阈值校准的可行性。结果显示合成数据在受控条件下表现良好，但在非受控环境中因分数分布尾部不匹配导致性能显著下降，最终结论是高风险安全部署仍需依赖真实世界数据进行阈值验证和调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟EES系统要求人脸识别在极低误匹配率下运行，但监管框架未明确成员国层面如何校准验证阈值。实际操作中获取代表性真实数据受限于法律、后勤和隐私约束，因此亟需探索合成数据作为替代方案的可行性。

Method: 通过分析合成数据集与真实数据集在文档到活体验证场景中真实分数和冒充分数分布的匹配程度，评估跨域校准阈值的可迁移性，重点关注低FMR工作点下的表现。

Result: 合成数据在受控设置下能近似校准行为，但在非受控条件下因分数分布尾部不匹配无法可靠泛化，导致识别性能显著下降且对形态攻击的脆弱性增加。校准结果具有高度数据集依赖性，即使在合成数据集之间也存在差异。

Conclusion: 合成数据对系统开发和初步校准有用，但在高安全部署中，可靠的阈值选择通常需要使用代表性真实世界数据进行验证和调整。

Abstract: The recently deployed Entry/Exit System (EES) introduces large-scale biometric verification into European border control, requiring face recognition systems to operate at extremely low false match rates (FMR). While regulatory frameworks define performance targets at the EES Central System level, they do not specify how verification thresholds should be calibrated in practice at the Member State level. In operational settings, obtaining representative real-world data for calibration is often constrained by legal, logistical, and privacy limitations.
  In this work, we investigate the use of synthetic face data for threshold calibration in document-to-live verification scenarios relevant to border control systems. We analyze the alignment of genuine and impostor score distributions between synthetic and real datasets and evaluate the transferability of calibrated thresholds across domains, with a focus on low-FMR operating points.
  Our results show that synthetic data can approximate calibration behavior in controlled settings, but fails to reliably generalize to unconstrained conditions due to mismatches in score distribution tails. These discrepancies lead to significant degradation in recognition performance and increased vulnerability to morph-based attacks. We further demonstrate that calibration outcomes are highly dataset-dependent, even across synthetic datasets.
  Overall, our findings highlight that while synthetic data is useful for system development and preliminary calibration, our results indicate that reliable threshold selection in high-security deployments typically requires validation and adjustment using representative real-world data.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Zooming: Learning Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25993)
*Fengxiang Wang,Jiangnan Huang,Mingshuo Chen,Yueying Li,Yang Shi,Junwei Luo,Haoyu Wang,Yansheng Li,Jing Zhang,Haiyan Zhao,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对超高分辨率遥感图像中多模态大模型面临证据稀疏、分散的挑战，通过pilot study发现单一缩放工具效果有限，进而提出大规模多工具视觉推理数据集GeoMTVR（含13K样本及推理轨迹）和工具注意力强化学习算法，最终构建GeoLens模型，在精度、证据定位和工具使用效率上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高分辨率遥感图像中任务相关证据稀疏、局部且分散于超大视觉上下文中，MLLMs难以有效处理。现有单一缩放工具虽能解决简单/中等任务，但在需全局搜索、多区域比较、路径规划或分散证据推理的困难任务上效果饱和，亟需多工具协同推理能力。

Method: 1）构建GeoMTVR数据集：包含13K UHR VQA样本，配有交错推理轨迹、多样化视觉工具调用及返回的视觉观测；2）提出工具注意力强化学习算法：聚焦优化关键工具使用决策（调用时机、工具选择、应用位置、输出解释）；3）将SFT与RL结合开发GeoLens模型。

Result: GeoLens在精度、证据定位和工具使用轨迹效率上均优于直接推理和单工具缩放基线方法，特别是在需要全局搜索、多区域比较等困难任务上表现更优。

Conclusion: 多工具视觉推理结合工具注意力强化学习是解决超高分辨率遥感图像中复杂视觉推理任务的有效范式，超越了单一缩放工具的局限。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote-sensing (RS) imagery provides fine-grained Earth-observation evidence over city-scale scenes, but poses a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs): task-relevant evidence is often sparse, local, and spatially dispersed across extremely large visual contexts. A natural solution is to equip MLLMs with zoom-in tools for active local inspection. However, through a pilot study on XLRS-Bench, we find that zoom-in is only partially effective: it resolves easy and medium-level tasks with locally recoverable evidence, but saturates on hard cases requiring global search, multi-region comparison, path planning, or dispersed-evidence reasoning. Motivated by this finding, we move beyond single-tool zoom-in and introduce GeoMTVR, a large-scale Geospatial Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning dataset built from wide-area satellite imagery. GeoMTVR contains 13K UHR VQA samples with interleaved reasoning trajectories, diverse visual tool calls, and returned visual observations, enabling models to learn question decomposition, tool selection, regional inspection, object-level grounding, auxiliary visual reasoning, and cross-tool evidence integration. Beyond supervised fine-tuning, we propose a tool-attention-focused reinforcement learning algorithm that concentrates optimization on critical tool-use decisions, including when to invoke tools, which tool to select, where to apply it, and how to interpret tool outputs. By combining SFT on GeoMTVR with our RL algorithm, we develop GeoLens, a multi-tool visual reasoning MLLM for UHR RS. Experiments show that GeoLens consistently outperforms direct reasoning and single-tool zoom-in baselines, achieving stronger accuracy, better evidence grounding, and more efficient tool-use trajectories.

</details>


### [85] [Parallel Decoding Distillation for Fast Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26004)
*Neta Shaul,Chao Liu,Arash Vahdat,Julius Berner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并行解码蒸馏（PDD），通过每步预测多个去噪步骤来加速视频扩散/流模型采样，以4-8次函数评估达到SOTA性能，同时提升生成多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散/流模型采样速度慢，SOTA加速方法（VSD、对抗蒸馏）优化困难且易模式崩溃，导致视频多样性损失和运动缺失。

Method: 提出并行解码蒸馏（PDD），一种轨迹级蒸馏方法：通过每步网络评估预测多个去噪步骤来加速推理；学习平均速度表示而不使用JVPs或有限差分近似回归导数；架构和训练流程兼容任意预训练模型，支持可变NFE采样。

Result: 在LTX-2.3 Text-to-Video/Audio、Wan 14B Text-to-Video和Qwen-Image Text-to-Image上，以4-8 NFE达到SOTA性能，并显著提升生成视频多样性。

Conclusion: PDD是一种简化、可扩展的轨迹级蒸馏方法，成功克服了VSD和对抗蒸馏的优化困难与模式崩溃问题，在加速推理的同时保持了生成质量与多样性。

Abstract: Generation in video diffusion or flow models is computationally expensive due to the slow and iterative sampling process. Current state-of-the-art (SOTA) acceleration methods heavily rely on variational score distillation (VSD) and adversarial losses to distill diffusion models into few-step generators. Albeit achieving high-quality video generation, these training losses are notoriously hard to optimize and suffer from mode collapse, leading to loss of video diversity and lack of motion. In this paper, we introduce Parallel Decoding Distillation (PDD), a simplified and scalable trajectory-based distillation method for fast inference of diffusion and flow matching models. Our architecture and training procedure are compatible with any pre-trained model and support sampling with a varying number of function evaluations (NFE). PDD accelerates generation by predicting multiple denoising steps per network evaluation. Conceptually, it learns a representation of the mean velocity without regressing its derivative using JVPs or finite-difference approximations. Our method achieves SOTA performance with 4-8 NFE on LTX-2.3 Text-to-Video/Audio, Wan 14B Text-to-Video, and Qwen-Image Text-to-Image. Moreover, PDD presents a significant improvement in generated video diversity.

</details>


### [86] [Pictura: Perspective-View Self-Play at Scale for Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26005)
*Yuan Yin,Elias Ramzi,Marc Lafon,Valentin Charraut,Victor Bares,Yihong Xu,Éloi Zablocki,Alexandre Boulch,Thibault Buhet,Andrei Bursuc,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Pictura——一个GPU加速的多智能体驾驶模拟器，通过渲染每个智能体的第一视角图像，从根本上消除特权观察与相机输入之间的表征差距。基于该模拟器，采用纯PPO算法对Alberti策略进行自博弈训练（500亿智能体步，约3500万公里驾驶），首次实现了直接从透视图像进行大规模驾驶自博弈训练，性能接近特权向量化策略，并在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上零样本迁移时超越后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶自博弈方法依赖特权向量化观察（如精确位姿和速度），假设感知问题已解决，导致部署时相机输入与训练时的特权观察之间存在表征差距。常见的特权蒸馏方法让学生模仿其自身视角无法证明的决策，无法从根本上解决问题。

Method: 1) 构建Pictura：GPU加速的多智能体驾驶模拟器，在每一步渲染每个智能体的第一视角图像，从根源上消除表征差距；2) 在Pictura中，使用标准PPO算法对Alberti策略进行自博弈训练，直接以透视图像为输入，不依赖任何特权观察。

Result: 1) Pictura在单个H100上支持500K agent-steps/s（2M images/s）；2) Alberti训练50B agent-steps，约3500万公里驾驶；3) Alberti性能接近特权向量化策略；4) 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上零样本迁移时，Alberti超越了特权向量化智能体。

Conclusion: 透视视图自博弈是一种实用且有效的训练范式，能够在不依赖特权观察的情况下学习鲁棒的驾驶策略，并通过零样本迁移展示了其泛化能力。Pictura模拟器和Alberti策略验证了这一范式的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Self-play in simulation produces robust driving policies at scale. Demonstrations of such behavior have been made using privileged vectorized observations such as exact poses and velocities, even for occluded agents. This assumes that perception is solved and introduces a representation gap with the partial observation of a deployed agent driving from the perspective view of egocentric cameras. A common fix, distilling the privileged policy into a camera-input student, leaves the student imitating decisions its own view cannot justify. Instead, we establish perspective-view self-play as a practical training regime. We introduce Pictura, a GPU-accelerated multi-agent driving simulator that renders each agent's egocentric view at every step, mitigating the representation gap at its source. Pictura sustains up to 500K agent-steps/s (2M images/s) on a single H100. Using Pictura, we train Alberti by self-play with plain PPO. It is the first large-scale driving self-play policy trained directly from perspective images, without privileged observations. Training spans 50B agent steps for ~35M km of driving. It approaches the driving performance of its privileged vectorized counterpart, and transfers zero-shot to Waymo Open Motion Dataset layouts re-rendered in Pictura, where it outperforms privileged vectorized agents. Project page: https://valeoai.github.io/Pictura/

</details>


### [87] [Wonder: Video World Model Done Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26037)
*Jiacong Xu,Hanwen Jiang,Zhixin Shu,Kalyan Sunkavalli,Vishal M. Patel,Yiqun Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wonder是一个通用视频世界模型，支持实时、相机可控的世界探索。它通过相机条件控制、稀疏注意力记忆机制和自强迫蒸馏改进技术，实现了在16FPS下生成分钟级连贯视频，允许用户交互式导航并重新拍摄动态场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型缺乏实时交互性和长期连贯性，用户无法在生成的世界中自由移动相机视角并探索未见区域。作者旨在构建一个可玩的、支持实时相机控制的通用视频世界模型。

Method: 1) 引入密集坐标场进行相机条件控制，提供空间对齐的运动和方向线索；2) 提出基于稀疏注意力的高效记忆机制，实现快速精确的上下文检索；3) 改进自强迫蒸馏流程，增强学生模型对控制信号的遵从性，同时保持教师模型的多样化生成模式和长期记忆。

Result: Wonder能在16FPS下合成多样化的分钟级视频，在长时间滚动生成中保持连贯的几何、外观和动态。支持图像到视频和视频条件生成两种模式。

Conclusion: 通过系统级的控制方法、记忆机制和训练策略协同设计，Wonder实现了实时、相机可控的长期视频世界探索，并自然支持现有动态场景的重新拍摄。

Abstract: We present Wonder, a general-purpose video world model for real-time, camera-controllable world exploration. Given an image or a conditional video, Wonder constructs a playable world where users can navigate interactively by moving the camera, discovering unseen regions, and revisiting previously observed areas in real time and over a long-term horizon. Achieving this capability requires a system-level co-design of control method, memory mechanism, and training strategy. We introduce a novel camera conditioning with a dense coordinate field whose renderings provide spatially aligned motion and orientation cues, allowing the model to interpret camera motion directly as visual evidence. To support fast and precise memory retrieval over a growing generation context, we propose an efficient sparse attention-based memory mechanism, enabling the model to selectively attend to a small set of relevant context tokens at inference time, regardless of actual context length. We further develop several techniques to rectify the self-forcing-style distillation pipeline, improving the student model's ability to respect control signals, as well as maintaining diverse generation modes and long-term memory from the teacher. Together, these components enable Wonder to synthesize diverse, minute-scale videos at 16 FPS while preserving coherent geometry, appearance, and dynamics across long rollouts. Beyond image-to-video generation, Wonder naturally supports video-conditioned generation, allowing existing dynamic scenes to be re-shot in real time.

</details>


### [88] [VetClaw: An Edge-Cloud Multimodal Agentic System for Veterinary Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26042)
*Syed Mhamudul Hasan,Anas AlSobeh,Hussein Zangoti,Abdur R. Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VetClaw是一个边缘-云多模态智能体系统，用于早期兽医疾病筛查。它通过边缘设备摄像头捕获图像，结合症状描述，由云端视觉语言模型进行零样本疾病分类。系统将智能体交互与工作流编排分离：OpenClaw负责边缘端调度、工具访问和通知，LangGraph管理状态化筛查工作流。实验表明，仅图像预测效果有限，而症状引导和多模态输入显著提升零样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将静态图像分类模型转变为具备工具使用、工作流管理、故障处理和不确定性升级能力的协调化、安全感知的智能系统，以解决早期兽医疾病筛查中传统静态模型无法处理多模态输入、缺乏工作流编排和安全机制的局限性。

Method: 提出边缘-云多模态智能体架构VetClaw，采用OpenClaw（边缘设备端）负责智能体交互、调度和通知，LangGraph（云端）管理状态化筛查工作流，包括输入验证、图像传输、模型调用、安全检查、条件路由、故障处理和结构化日志。支持图像、症状描述及多模态输入的零样本疾病分类。

Result: 仅图像的VLM预测性能有限，而症状引导和多模态输入显著提升了零样本分类性能。系统能够收集视觉证据、调用外部模型、应用确定性安全规则并生成诊断支持警报。

Conclusion: VetClaw将静态预测模型转化为协调化、安全感知的系统，能够使用工具、管理工作流、处理故障并对不确定病例进行升级处理，为早期兽医疾病筛查提供了可行的边缘-云协同解决方案。

Abstract: We present VetClaw, an edge-cloud multimodal agentic system for early veterinary disease screening. VetClaw uses a camera module as an edge sensing device and sends captured images, together with optional symptom descriptions, to a server-hosted vision-language model for zero-shot disease classification. The system separates agent interaction from workflow orchestration: OpenClaw provides scheduling, tool access, user interaction, and notification services on the edge device, while LangGraph manages the stateful screening workflow, including input validation, image transmission, model invocation, safety checks, conditional routing, failure handling, and structured logging. This design moves beyond static image classification by enabling the system to collect visual evidence, invoke external models, apply deterministic safety rules, and generate diagnostic-support alerts. Results show that image-only VLM prediction remains limited, whereas symptom-guided and multimodal inputs improve zero-shot classification performance. Thus, VetClaw transforms a static prediction model into a coordinated, safety-aware system that can use tools, manage workflows, handle failures, and escalate uncertain cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [FinAbstain: Uncertainty-Calibrated Multimodal RAG for Selective Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24875)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Henan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FinAbstain框架，一种面向金融预测的不确定性校准多模态检索增强生成（RAG）与选择性预测方法。该框架通过时间点检索器确保信息时效性，聚合多智能体概率评估，结合多种校准技术（温度缩放、保序回归、共形预测及混合不确定性评分），在不确定性低于阈值时输出预测，否则选择弃权。评估覆盖多个时间窗口和指标，展示了校准弃权策略在降低选择性误差和回撤方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在金融叙事生成中常因证据稀疏、过时或矛盾而表现出过度自信，这在预测场景中尤为严重。现有方法未能有效处理多模态金融信号之间的不一致性，且缺乏系统性的不确定性校准和选择性预测机制。

Method: FinAbstain框架包含：（1）时间点检索器，仅采纳预测时间戳前的公开信息；（2）多智能体系统，包括基本面、新闻、技术、风险和验证智能体，各自生成概率评估；（3）聚合机制，融合检索相关性、证据矛盾性、重复采样一致性和历史校准统计量；（4）不确定性校准模块，评估温度缩放、保序回归、共形预测及提出的混合不确定性评分；（5）控制器，在不确定性低于阈值时输出看涨/看跌/中性预测，否则弃权、请求证据、降低暴露或转交人工审核。

Result: 在1天和5天异常收益方向、20天波动率区间及弃权决策上，使用准确率、校准度、风险-覆盖率、引用、交易、延迟和成本指标进行评估。报告了明确标注的模拟结果（而非经验性声明），表明校准弃权策略可以用覆盖率换取更低的选择性误差和回撤。

Conclusion: 本文贡献包括：时间安全的架构设计、复合不确定性公式、以及可复现的证据驱动选择性金融预测评估蓝图。校准弃权策略能够有效平衡预测覆盖率与误差/风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can synthesize financial narratives but may express high confidence when evidence is sparse, stale, or contradictory. This failure is especially consequential in forecasting, where filings, news, prices, volume, and technical signals can disagree. We present FinAbstain, a research framework for uncertainty-calibrated multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with selective prediction. A point-in-time retriever admits only information public at the forecast timestamp and supplies modality-specific evidence to fundamental, news, technical, risk, and verification agents. Their probabilistic assessments are aggregated with retrieval relevance, evidence contradiction, repeated-sample consistency, and historical calibration statistics. Temperature scaling, isotonic regression, conformal prediction, and a proposed hybrid uncertainty score are evaluated under a common chronological protocol. A controller predicts bullish, bearish, or neutral outcomes only when uncertainty is below a validated threshold; otherwise it abstains, requests evidence, reduces exposure, or routes the case to human review. The evaluation covers one- and five-day abnormal-return direction, twenty-day volatility intervals, and abstention decisions, using accuracy, calibration, risk--coverage, citation, trading, latency, and cost metrics. To make the design auditable before a full data collection is complete, we report explicitly labeled simulated results rather than empirical claims. These results illustrate the intended hypothesis: calibrated abstention may trade coverage for lower selective error and drawdown. The contribution is a time-safe architecture, a composite uncertainty formulation, and a reproducible evaluation blueprint for evidence-grounded selective financial forecasting.

</details>


### [90] [Human Preference aligned Tabular Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24880)
*Frederik Hoppe,Astrid Franz,Marianne Michaelis,Lars Kleinemeier,Udo Göbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前任务无关的表格嵌入方法主要针对预测任务优化，而非产生符合人类偏好的相似性排序，导致在PLM等系统的相似性检索中评估不足。作者提出需要引入人类偏好对齐的评估方法，并给出了具体评估流程和PLM用例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格嵌入方法在相似性搜索任务中缺乏人类偏好对齐的评估标准，标准下游指标不足以评估嵌入的可靠性，亟需补充这一缺失环节。

Method: 提出一个具体的人类偏好对齐评估流程，并通过PLM用例进行问题说明。

Result: 证明了标准下游指标不足以评估相似性搜索中嵌入的可信度，指出人类偏好对齐的评估是必要且缺失的组成部分。

Conclusion: 任务无关表格嵌入用于相似性搜索时，需要引入人类偏好对齐的评估方法，以确保嵌入排序结果符合实际使用需求。

Abstract: Task-agnostic tabular embeddings are increasingly used for similarity search in real-world business systems such as Product Lifecycle Management (PLM). However, leading embedding approaches are optimized primarily for prediction tasks - not for producing human preference aligned similarity rankings. We argue that standard downstream metrics are insufficient to fully assess embedding trustworthiness for similarity search and that human preference aligned evaluation is a necessary and currently missing component. We present a concrete evaluation procedure and illustrate the problem through a PLM use case.

</details>


### [91] [Behavior-Driven Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24881)
*Caroline Dominik,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于行为驱动开发（BDD）的场景化规约来生成系统行为解释的方法（BDX），并在RISC-V处理器异常场景中进行案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着系统复杂度急剧增加，单个人员或团队难以全面理解系统全生命周期（如设计、维护）的所有方面。尤其在安全关键系统中，最终设计的可信度至关重要，因此可解释性成为现代系统的重要需求。

Method: 利用行为驱动开发（BDD）的结构化场景来描述期望的系统行为，每个功能对应一系列动作序列，这些场景可直接转化为解释。该方法被称为行为驱动可解释性（BDX），可应用于任何开发阶段或抽象层级。

Result: 通过RISC-V处理器中异常解释的案例研究，验证了BDX方法在系统设计阶段提供解释支持的有效性。

Conclusion: BDX方法基于BDD规约生成解释，适用于系统全生命周期，为提升安全关键系统的可理解性和可信度提供了可行途径。

Abstract: As system complexity has vastly increased, it has become significantly more challenging for a single person or a team to fully understand all aspects of an entire system. Particularly, this holds when considering all the different stages of a system's development life cycle, such as, e.g., design or maintenance. But especially for safety-critical systems it is essential that the final design can be trusted. Because of this, explainability is becoming an important requirement for modern systems. In this paper, we aim to achieve this goal by utilizing Behavior-Driven Development (BDD), where the expected system behavior is given in the form of structured scenarios. These scenarios give a sequence of actions for each functionality, and by this can be directly translated into explanations. We introduce this method of deriving explanations based on the specification as Behavior-Driven Explainability (BDX). While applicable at any development stage or abstraction level, a case study for the explanation of exceptions in a RISC-V processor shows the support this concept adds during system design.

</details>


### [92] [Multiclass Classification without Labels via Posterior Simplex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24943)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多类别无标签分类（MCWoLa）方法，将二分类中的CWoLa原理推广到K>2的多类场景。该方法利用不同混合比例的无标签混合数据，通过训练区分混合身份的判别器，再基于混合后验空间的(K-1)-单纯形几何结构提取潜在类别信息。无需先验类别标签或混合矩阵即可恢复最优分类器，在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Galaxy10 DECaLS上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在众多分类问题中，可靠的实例级标签难以获取，但常可构建弱增广的无标签样本（如不同筛选条件、来源或实验条件下的数据集）。二分类场景中CWoLa已证明可从不纯混合中恢复最优分类器，但多类别场景（K>2）尚未有理论和方法支撑。本文旨在将这一原理推广到多类别学习，弥合弱监督与完全监督学习之间的性能差距。

Method: 1) 理论证明了多类别混合模型下，贝叶斯最优混合分类器g*将数据点映射到混合后验空间中的(K-1)-单纯形，其K个顶点由潜在类别通过未知混合矩阵诱导；2) 提出两种无先验流程：后验单纯形拟合（post-hoc simplex fitting）和瓶颈架构（bottleneck architecture），通过训练标准分类器区分混合身份后提取潜在类别结构。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Galaxy10 DECaLS数据集上的实验表明，仅利用混合身份信息即可有效恢复潜在类别及其在混合中的比例，弱监督性能接近完全监督水平。

Conclusion: 本文提供了数学基础扎实且可扩展的多类别发现工具，适用于标签稀缺领域，显著缩小了弱监督与完全监督学习之间的差距。

Abstract: In many classification problems, reliable instance-level labels are unavailable. However, it is often possible to construct weakly enriched unlabeled samples: datasets selected by different cuts, sources, populations, or experimental conditions that change latent class proportions without revealing them. Classification without Labels (CWoLa) shows that, in the binary case ($K=2$), a classifier trained to distinguish two impure mixtures with different class proportions can recover an optimal class discriminator without knowing the mixture proportions. We extend this principle to multiclass learning from several unlabeled mixtures ($K>2$), where the learner observes only mixture identity and neither latent class labels nor class-prior matrices. We prove that, for a multiclass mixture model, the Bayes-optimal mixture classifier $g^\star$ maps data points into a $(K-1)$-simplex embedded in mixture-posterior space. The $K$ vertices of this simplex are induced by the latent classes through the unknown mixing matrix. Leveraging this geometry, we propose prior-free procedures that train a standard classifier to distinguish mixture identities and then extract latent class structure using either post-hoc simplex fitting or a bottleneck architecture. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Galaxy10 DECaLS show that mixture identity alone can recover latent classes and their fractions in the mixture. By narrowing the gap between weakly supervised and fully supervised performance, we provide a mathematically grounded, scalable tool for multiclass discovery in label-scarce domains.

</details>


### [93] [Eliminating Propagation Delay: Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24885)
*Jinpeng Chen,Ziyu Yu,Tao Wang,Jun Ma,Hongbo Gao,Senzhang Wang,Zufeng Zhang,Kaimin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为A-STFGCN的注意力时空融合图卷积网络，通过设计时空融合模块，消除传播延迟误差，并利用基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕获长短期时间特征，在五个真实数据集上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图卷积模型主要关注拓扑结构中的静态空间依赖和时空关系，忽视了相邻节点间信息传播的延迟差异；同时，复杂模型堆叠导致训练时间过长，忽略了模型的时效性需求。

Method: 提出A-STFGCN网络，设计时空融合模块提取消除传播延迟误差的时空特征相关性，并利用基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕获数据的长期和短期时间特征。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，与八种基线方法相比，该方法在取得最佳整体性能的同时，具有良好的计算和数据利用效率。

Conclusion: A-STFGCN通过解决传播延迟和时效性问题，在交通流预测任务上实现了性能与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Predicting traffic flow is crucial to optimizing transportation systems and improving urban mobility. Many graph convolution-based models have been proposed to extract spatial-temporal features and predict traffic flow. However, most focus on spatial-temporal and semantic correlation in topological relationships. There are two primary problems to address. Firstly, the convolutional structure in the model focuses on utilizing static spatial dependencies and spatial-temporal relationships in topological structures, while neglecting the different information propagation delays between adjacent nodes in the convolution. Secondly, these methods often stack a large number of complex structures, resulting in a substantial increase in computational time during the model training phase, thereby disregarding the model's requirements for timeliness. In this paper, we propose a novel network called the Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network (A-STFGCN). We design a spatial-temporal fusion block to extract the spatial-temporal feature correlations with propagation delay errors removed and to capture both long-term and short-term temporal characteristics of the data within a multi-head self-attention mechanism based on a mask matrix. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves the best overall performance while having good computation and data utilization efficiency compared with the eight baseline methods.

</details>


### [94] [Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24983)
*Ziwei Zhang,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li,Zhihao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式分布鲁棒优化（GDRO）框架，通过采样器-Sinkhorn配对，在无需似然函数的情况下，将最坏情况分布限制在条件生成器族内，实现了有限样本近似和可微的原偶优化，显著降低了稀有场景下的库存遗憾和导航碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在分布鲁棒优化中要么接受任意采样器但不限制最坏情况分布，要么依赖模型特定的似然、分数或训练数据来参数化对抗分布，缺乏一个既能接受任意可采样条件生成器作为名义模型、又能将最坏情况限制在选定生成器族内的统一框架。

Method: 提出GDRO框架，核心是采样器-Sinkhorn配对：采样器精确表示条件分布，Sinkhorn散度无需似然即可比较分布差异。问题可转化为直接有限样本近似，并在决策上下文中实现可微的原偶优化。

Result: 对于Lipschitz损失，种群Sinkhorn半径约束了下游退化。在显式和隐式生成器上，该方法将稀有上下文库存遗憾降低60%，SocialGAN导航碰撞减少50%。

Conclusion: GDRO提供了一个通用、高效且无需似然访问的分布鲁棒优化框架，在生成模型兼容性和对抗结构之间取得了良好的平衡，在实际应用中显著提升了鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly adopted in distributionally robust optimization (DRO), but existing approaches trade off model compatibility and adversarial structure: methods that accept arbitrary samplers do not restrict worst-case laws to a generator family, while generator-parameterized adversaries rely on model-specific access such as likelihoods, scores, or training data. We propose Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization (GDRO), a principled framework that accepts any sampleable conditional generator as the nominal model and restricts worst-case laws to a chosen conditional generator family. The key is the sampler-Sinkhorn pairing: samplers represent the conditional laws exactly, while Sinkhorn divergence compares their induced distributions without likelihood access and can be estimated from samples alone. The resulting population problem admits a direct finite-sample approximation and differentiable primal-dual implementation at the active decision context. For Lipschitz losses, the population Sinkhorn radius bounds downstream degradation. Across explicit and implicit generators, our method reduces rare-context inventory regret by 60% and SocialGAN navigation collisions by 50% relative to nominal decisions.

</details>


### [95] [Mechanisms of Width Scaling in Normalized Residual Networks: The Effective Alignment Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24887)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Guangming Tan,Fangming Liu,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对残差扩展网络，提出了有效对齐维度这一可测量指标，用于描述激活梯度的信号-噪声几何结构。通过推导训练与测试梯度内积的精确均值和方差，得到了错位概率的有限样本上界，仅依赖于有效对齐维度和有效样本量。实验表明，更宽的模型具有更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验错位，且对齐统计量可预测留出损失变化的符号和幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络宽度理论局限于渐近极限分析，无法回答有限训练数据下识别出的扩展方向是否在未见数据上仍然有益。本文旨在解决这一实际问题。

Method: 定义有效对齐维度描述激活梯度的信号-噪声几何结构；推导训练与测试梯度内积的精确均值和方差；建立错位概率的有限样本上界，仅需有限二阶矩和非零总体梯度，无需协方差谱假设或预设宽度增长率；将该上界融入训练-测试残差扩展框架，得到测试风险改进的高概率条件。

Result: 在宽度控制的LLaMA风格Transformer、Pythia和ResNet-20上，更宽的模型表现出更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验错位。直接残差干预实验证实对齐统计量可预测留出损失变化的符号和幅度。

Conclusion: 有效对齐维度是衡量有限样本下残差扩展方向泛化能力的实用指标，为预测网络宽度扩展对测试性能的影响提供了可操作的理论保证。

Abstract: Existing theories of neural-network width characterize asymptotic limits, but provide limited guidance on whether an expansion direction identified from finite training data remains beneficial on unseen data. We study this problem for function-preserving residual expansion and introduce the effective alignment dimension, a measurable quantity describing the signal-noise geometry of activation gradients. By deriving the exact mean and variance of the inner product between independently estimated training and test gradients, we obtain a finite-sample upper bound on misalignment probability. The bound depends only on the effective alignment dimension and an effective sample size, requiring finite second moments and a nonzero population gradient, without covariance spectral assumptions or prescribed width-growth rates. We integrate this certificate into the train-test residual-expansion framework, yielding a high-probability condition for test-risk improvement. Experiments across width-controlled LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia, and ResNet-20 show that wider models exhibit larger effective alignment dimensions and lower empirical misalignment. Direct residual interventions confirm that the alignment statistic predicts the sign and magnitude of held-out loss changes.

</details>


### [96] [GAUGE: Grading Agent-Built Financial Models Without a Golden Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24889)
*Jiacheng Lu,Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Rui Sun,Cheng Hua,Tao Song,Hui Cai,Beidi Luan,Zhengze Wu,Lingjing Teng,Yijia He,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAUGE基准，用于评估基于代理的估值模型，通过对标真实分析师实践而非单一参考答案。研究发现传统点容差评分会惩罚专业人员间已有的分歧，而GAUGE通过三层观察实践包络、56个可审计层面、8个有效性门控和确定性结构检查，提供了更合理的评估框架。最佳代理得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有资深分析师和大多数初级分析师，显示当前代理在模型构建方面强于估值判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融模型基准通常将输出与单一专家参考答案进行对比评分，但分析师之间本身就存在合理的意见分歧（如预测、贴现率、目标价等）。使用独立构建的分析师模型进行验证发现，单参考评分中位数仅0.33，92.6%的评分低于0.70，且同一时期的分析师对内在价格的一致率不超过10%，说明点容差评分会不公平地惩罚专业人员间已有的分歧。

Method: GAUGE基准包含1,001个供应商分类的分析师工作簿和196项评估任务，采用三层观察实践包络、56个可审计层面、8个有效性门控和确定性结构检查。通过55名参与者的已知群体研究、公司分组交叉拟合和判断稳定性审计进行验证。使用失败感知评分φ₀评估，并对24个代理进行了1,011次评分生成测试。

Result: 资深分析师平均得分88.3，初级分析师66.0，金融专业学生43.2。最佳代理得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有资深分析师和大多数初级分析师。代理通过93%的机械层面和78%的判断层面，舰队中位数差距26分。

Conclusion: 当前代理在模型构建（机械层面）方面表现较强，但在估值判断（判断层面）方面明显不足，能力显著低于资深分析师和多数初级分析师。GAUGE基准为评估金融AI代理提供了更符合实际行业实践的评测框架，并公开了方法论、去标识化数据、训练集、版本化48任务评估核心及 withheld 刷新池。

Abstract: Financial models combine public disclosures with analyst assumptions to produce forecasts and valuations. While some components can be checked mechanically, forecasts, discount rates, and target prices often admit multiple reasonable answers. Existing benchmarks nevertheless tend to grade such outputs against a single expert reference. Using independently built analyst models for the same companies, we find that across 108 directed pairs covering 65 companies, the median single-reference score is 0.33, 92.6% score below 0.70, and no same-vintage pair agrees on implied price within 10%. Point-tolerance grading can therefore penalize disagreement already present among professionals. We introduce GAUGE, a benchmark for evaluating agent-built valuation models against observed analyst practice rather than a single point answer. GAUGE uses 1,001 vendor-classified analyst workbooks and a 196-task evaluation set, with a three-layer observed-practice envelope, 56 auditable facets, eight validity gates, and deterministic structural checks. We validate the benchmark with a 55-participant known-groups study, company-grouped cross-fitting, and judge-stability audits. On the failure-aware score $φ_0$, senior analysts average 88.3, juniors 66.0, and finance students 43.2. Across 24 agents and 1,011 scored generations, the best agent scores 53.4, above the student mean but below every senior and most juniors. It passes 93% of mechanical facets and 78% of judgment facets, with a fleet-median gap of 26 points. Current agents are substantially stronger at model construction than valuation judgment. We release the methodology, a gated de-identified data tier, a controlled training split, a versioned 48-task evaluation core, and a withheld refresh pool.

</details>


### [97] [Algorithmic Separation between Constant-Depth and Logarithmic-Depth Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25200)
*Yunwei Ren,Zihao Wang,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了常数深度网络与对数深度网络之间存在算法上的分离：对于一类具有分层傅里叶谱结构的布尔函数，对数深度网络可以通过逐层坐标下降高效学习，而常数深度网络在特定条件下会遭遇恒定的近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在实践中优于浅层网络，但之前的理论结果主要集中于逼近能力上的深度分离，而算法层面的结论大多局限于两层与三层网络的比较。本文旨在证明更深（对数深度）网络在可学习性上相对于常数深度网络的优势。

Method: 1. 识别了一类具有层级化傅里叶谱结构的布尔函数。2. 证明对数深度网络可通过逐层坐标下降（layerwise coordinate descent）分层自适应地重构傅里叶谱，从而高效学习。3. 构造了一个子类，证明任何具有足够正则激活函数和受控谱范数的常数深度、多项式宽度网络，在超立方体均匀分布下必然产生恒定的L^2近似误差。

Result: 首次在算法层面上证明了常数深度网络与对数深度网络之间的分离：对数深度网络可高效学习该类函数，而常数深度网络无法达到任意小的近似误差。

Conclusion: 深度不仅在逼近意义上重要，在可学习性/算法意义上同样关键。对数深度网络能够利用层级结构进行自适应的谱重建，而常数深度网络受限于其表达能力，无法克服这一计算-统计鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite the empirical advantages of deep networks over shallow ones, theoretical depth separations largely concern approximation power, while algorithmic results are mostly limited to comparisons between two- and three-layer networks. In this work, we prove the first algorithmic separation between constant-depth and logarithmic-depth networks.
  Specifically, we identify a class of Boolean functions with hierarchically structured Fourier spectra that logarithmic-depth networks can learn efficiently using layerwise coordinate descent by reconstructing the spectra hierarchically and adaptively. We also exhibit a subclass for which every constant-depth, polynomial-width network with sufficiently regular activations and controlled spectral norms must incur constant $L^2$ approximation error under the uniform distribution over the hypercube.

</details>


### [98] [LLM as Forecasting Planner: Training-Free Text Conditioning for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24892)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAFP框架，将文本条件时间序列预测转化为规划问题，利用冻结的TSFM作为模拟器生成数值轨迹，LLM作为策略和价值函数通过MCTS进行搜索，无需训练即可桥接两种模型的模态差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFM）擅长数值预测，大语言模型（LLM）能理解文本上下文，但直接让LLM生成或修改预测值会破坏TSFM捕获的时间结构，现有方法难以有效结合两者优势。

Method: 将预测建模为规划问题：冻结TSFM作为模拟器生成连续数值轨迹，LLM分为Ranker（策略）和Judge（价值函数），通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在预测时域上搜索最优轨迹，无需重新训练任何模型。

Result: 在Context-is-Key和Time-MMD数据集上，使用Chronos和TimesFM作为TSFM骨干、四种不同LLM，LAFP在所有模型组合下均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 序列搜索是一种有效的免训练文本条件预测方法，LAFP框架成功桥接了TSFM和LLM的模态差异，为文本条件时间序列预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-conditioned time-series forecasting predicts a series from both its numerical history and natural-language context, allowing forecasts to account for events and constraints that the past alone cannot reveal. This requires both reliable numerical forecasting and the ability to interpret contextual information. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide strong numerical forecasts, while large language models (LLMs) can reason over text, but combining their strengths remains challenging because asking an LLM to generate or revise forecast values directly can distort the temporal structure captured by the TSFM. We instead formulate forecasting as a planning problem over TSFM-generated trajectories. The frozen TSFM acts as a simulator that proposes numerical continuations, while the LLM acts as a policy and value function that guides candidate selection and evaluates completed trajectories against the context. We instantiate this as \rc{} (\textbf{L}LM \textbf{A}s \textbf{F}orecasting \textbf{P}lanner), a training-free framework that bridges the modality gap without retraining either model, using Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) over the forecast horizon with a \emph{Ranker} LLM as policy and a \emph{Judge} LLM as value function. Experiments on Context-is-Key and Time-MMD across two TSFM backbones (Chronos and TimesFM) and four LLMs show that \rc{} delivers consistent improvements across model choices, supporting sequential search as an effective training-free approach to text-conditioned forecasting.

</details>


### [99] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Contextual Deconvolution (CD)方法，通过凸分解将需求感知重构为核调制带状算子，分离促销驱动的瞬态冲击与平滑结构基线。在30,490个M5和2,845个Favorita SKU上评估，CD降低了安全库存、持有成本和订单方差，但会低估事件峰值。其核心贡献是预测可靠性（最低的跨截面误差离散度）而非中心趋势。当持有成本超过缺货成本的约20%时，CD降低总成本；否则作为运营稳定性层发挥作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习需求预测虽优化了统计精度，但遗留了过多的运营波动性，导致安全库存膨胀并放大牛鞭效应。现有方法未能有效分离促销驱动的瞬态冲击与结构性基线，且难以在大规模SKU目录上高效部署。

Method: 提出Contextual Deconvolution (CD)两阶段估计器：(1) 核调制带状算子（kernel-modulated banded operator）将需求感知重构为凸分解问题，分离瞬态促销冲击与平滑结构基线；(2) 层次化部分池化（hierarchical partial pooling）实现目录级部署，无需逐SKU训练。该算子由数据驱动，在促销响应为脉冲式时退化为恒等映射。

Result: 在30,490个M5 SKU和2,845个Favorita商品上严格样本外评估：CD降低了安全库存、持有成本和订单方差，但会低估事件峰值。在11个基线方法中，CD取得了最低的跨截面每SKU误差离散度和最低的误报率（0.8% SKU误报超200%，vs.基线9.9-20.6%），在M5四个数据划分中均排名第一。当持有成本超过缺货成本的约20%（95% CI [17%,25%]）时，CD降低总成本。

Conclusion: CD的准确性贡献在于可靠性而非中心趋势。方差比率和基于标准差的安全库存会被任何足够平滑的预测所最小化，因此应作为诊断指标而非优化目标。学习得到的需求算子是非正态的，但CD的紧凑参数化核能以可解释的方式匹配其操作性能。

Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

</details>


### [100] [Inverse RL Helps Align AI by Imitating Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24900)
*Michał Wiliński,Liu Leqi,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PARED从演示中恢复隐式奖励函数，通过对少量响应级特征进行轻量级判别学习，实现无需偏好标注的AI对齐，并在推理时重排和对抗性在线强化学习中提升基础策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索仅凭演示数据能否提取出可检查、可重用且可在策略上优化的隐式奖励，以对齐AI行为，弥补监督微调和强化学习范式之间的空白。

Method: 基于逆强化学习，学习一个轻量级判别器，在少量响应级特征空间中区分演示样本与策略自身样本，从而将演示背后的隐式奖励恢复为显式函数。

Result: 恢复的奖励在推理时重排和对抗性在线强化学习中提升了基础策略，在标准监督微调后进一步优化仍有增益，并支持面向不同受众的上下文对齐。

Conclusion: 仅凭演示即可恢复有效的隐式奖励用于对齐，无需任务特定的偏好标注，且该奖励可检查、可重用、可在线优化。

Abstract: Language model alignment aims to make model behavior reliably reflect desirable properties such as helpfulness, safety, and instruction following. Current approaches typically use supervised fine-tuning on demonstrations or reinforcement learning with rewards derived from verifiers or human feedback. These paradigms leave an important question underexplored: can demonstrations alone yield an implicit reward that can be inspected, reused, and optimized on-policy to align AI? Motivated by inverse reinforcement learning, we introduce Projected Alignment Reward Estimated from Demonstrations (PARED). PARED recovers the implicit reward underlying expert demonstrations as an explicit function over a small set of response-level features, learned by a lightweight discriminator that separates demonstrations from the policy's own samples in this feature space. Unlike a standard reward model, PARED requires no task-specific preference annotations: demonstrations provide the task-specific supervision, which can be augmented with AI feedback as additional dimensions of supervision. Through experiments involving inference-time reranking and adversarial on-policy RL, we show that the recovered reward improves a base policy without a supervised loss and yields further gains when optimized after standard supervised fine-tuning. Additionally, we demonstrate that PARED can be used for contextual alignment, in which a single policy can be tailored to the preferences of different audiences.

</details>


### [101] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将矩阵感知几何（谱内扰动）引入SAM的两阶段优化中，并与Muon优化器结合，在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50实现最佳验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM变体虽探索了多种几何选择，但何种几何最有效仍不清晰；受Muon优化器（尊重隐藏层权重的矩阵结构）的启发，研究矩阵感知几何在SAM两个阶段中的作用。

Method: 对矩阵值隐藏层参数提出逐层谱内扰动（spectral inner perturbation），将其与AdamW/SGDW或Muon作为外更新步骤相结合，形成矩阵感知的SAM变体。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50的实验表明，谱内步骤与Muon外步骤的组合在所有评估方法中持续表现最佳，取得最高验证精度。

Conclusion: 矩阵感知几何（谱内扰动）结合Muon优化器能够有效提升SAM的泛化性能，为SAM的几何选择提供了新的有效方向。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [102] [Reinformed Dreamer: An Asymmetric World Model Efficiently Trained through Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26040)
*Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Daniel Ebi,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reinformed Dreamer算法，通过潜空间引导的新型非对称表示学习，改进了非对称模型强化学习中特权信息表示的限制，在多个基准上比Dreamer和先前非对称方法表现更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现现有非对称模型算法Informed Dreamer在特权信息表示学习上存在局限性，需要更有效的非对称表示学习方法。

Method: 提出Reinformed Dreamer算法，采用潜空间引导的（latent guidance）新型非对称表示学习目标，在模型强化学习框架下同时优化观察表示和特权信息表示。

Result: 在多个基准实验中，Reinformed Dreamer相比Dreamer和先前的非对称方法（如Informed Dreamer）取得了更一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 潜空间引导的非对称表示学习能更有效地利用特权信息，为模型强化学习中的表示学习提供了改进方向。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning. This learning paradigm has proven effective under partial observability when additional state information is available, but also under full observability when more refined state information is available. Focusing on model-based reinforcement learning, we study the effect of asymmetric learning on observation representations and on privileged information representations. First, we identify a limitation in the privileged information representations learned by an asymmetric model-based algorithm known as the Informed Dreamer. Then, we propose a novel asymmetric representation learning objective using latent guidance, resulting in a new algorithm called the Reinformed Dreamer. Experiments across several benchmarks show a more consistent improvement over Dreamer than previous asymmetric approaches.

</details>


### [103] [Stable FP4 Training via Transposition-Invariant Block Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24953)
*Mehdi Rahimifar,Amin Darabi,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Xing Huang,Yao Wang,Zhijun Tu,Yufei Cui,Yunke Peng,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于2D块FP4量化的低精度训练框架，通过确保前向-后向缩放一致性来解决FP4训练中的不稳定性问题，在最高7B参数密集模型和30B MoE模型上实现了与BF16精度相当的训练效果（性能损失<1.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将训练精度从FP8推进到FP4存在优化不稳定性问题，现有1D块量化方法由于张量转置导致前向和后向传播为相同值分配不同的缩放因子，产生有偏且不稳定的梯度更新。

Method: 提出2D块FP4量化框架，核心设计包括：(1) 二维块量化实现转置不变缩放，保持前向-后向计算的一致性；(2) 无截断缩放与随机舍入控制量化误差并保持无偏梯度；(3) 对注意力机制的Q/K投影采用MXFP8量化的混合精度设计。

Result: 在7B参数密集模型和30B MoE模型上训练至100B tokens，端到端FP4训练稳定，困惑度和下游准确率相比BF16基线退化小于1.3%。

Conclusion: 确保前向-后向缩放一致性足以实现可规模化的FP4训练，为更高效的大语言模型训练提供了简单有效的路径。

Abstract: Reducing training precision is a key lever for improving the e ciency of large language model (LLM) training, but pushing beyond FP8 to 4-bit oating point (FP4) remains challenging due to instability during optimization. We identify a fundamental source of this instability in existing microscaling approaches: scale inconsistency induced by tensor transposition. In conventional 1D block quantization, forward and backward passes assign di erent scaling factors to the same values after transposition, leading to biased and unstable gradient updates. To address this issue, we propose a low-precision training framework based on 2D block FP4 quantization, which enforces transposition-invariant scaling and preserves consistency between forward and backward computations.
